We probe this dependence using targeted post-training low-pass interventions on internal representations of three segmentation architectures, ResNet50-UNet (CNN), VM-UNet (SSM), and Swin-UNETR (Transformer), across CVC-ClinicDB and ISIC2018, with headline evaluations performed on untouched held-out test sets. At cutoff rho=0.25, feature-domain low-pass filtering causes severe degradation on CVC: Dice drops by 100%, 73.2%, and 30.9% for CNN, SSM, and Transformer, respectively, compared with 9.4%, 10.3%, and 0.6% on ISIC. The cross-dataset difference is statistically significant for every architecture. Single-stage interventions further show that sensitivity is localized at architecture-specific depths: the CNN peaks at a mid/late encoder block, whereas the SSM peaks in an early encoder stage on both datasets. Native feature-domain spectral measurements show an inverse association between high-frequency energy and fragility on CVC; the relationship is only partial on ISIC and is therefore treated as a candidate correlate rather than a proven mechanism. Finally, Fourier augmentation improves robustness to input-space low-pass filtering but leaves feature-domain degradation essentially unchanged. These results show that feature-spectral robustness is strongly dataset-dependent, architecture-specific, and distinct from input-domain spectral robustness.

</details>


### [69] [A Tensor Variational Formulation of Gradient Energy Total Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29172)
*Freddie Åström,George Baravdish,Michael Felsberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于张量的全变分新变分方法——梯度能量全变分（GETV），将梯度能量张量引入GETV，证明了其对应的欧拉-拉格朗日方程为张量型全变分偏微分方程，并证明了GETV是凸泛函，实验表明其在灰度与彩色图像去噪上优于扩展各向异性扩散（EAD）和全变分（TV）等先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的结构张量方法无法形式化推导对应的欧拉-拉格朗日方程，限制了张量型变分方法在图像去噪等任务中的理论分析与应用推广。

Method: 提出一种新颖的变分方法——梯度能量全变分（GETV），将梯度能量张量引入全变分框架，推导出对应的张量型欧拉-拉格朗日方程，并给出GETV为凸泛函的严格数学证明。

Result: 实验结果表明，GETV在灰度图像和彩色图像去噪任务上均优于扩展各向异性扩散（EAD）和全变分（TV）等当前最优的变分去噪方法。

Conclusion: GETV作为一种基于梯度能量张量的凸变分模型，不仅具备形式化推导欧拉-拉格朗日方程的理论优势，还展现出优于现有先进变分去噪方法的性能。

Abstract: We present a novel variational approach to a tensor-based total variation formulation which is called gradient energy total variation, GETV. We introduce the gradient energy tensor [6] into the GETV and show that the corresponding Euler-Lagrange (E-L) equation is a tensor-based partial differential equation of total variation type. Furthermore, we give a proof which shows that GETV is a convex functional. This approach, in contrast to the commonly used structure tensor, enables a formal derivation of the corresponding E-L equation. Experimental results suggest that GETV compares favourably to other state of the art variational denoising methods such as extended anisotropic diffusion (EAD)[1] and total variation (TV) [18] for gray-scale and colour images.

</details>


### [70] [Dynamic-Robust Photometric-Semantic Reconstruction for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29177)
*Boyu Cai,Li Yang,Yan Xu,Wei Liu,Nian Liu,Sikui Zhang,Yan Wang,Chunfeng Yuan,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPAR，一种联合语义-几何编码架构，通过显式分离瞬态动态噪声、引入动态区域感知的端到端训练范式，将运动估计与多视角视觉和语义学习结构化耦合，从而在动态场景中实现新视角合成与开放词汇分割的统一。在D-RE10K基准上达到SOTA性能（PSNR 22.15/23.33 dB，分别给定3/4视角；运动掩码mIoU 88.5%），并发现光度重建与语义理解之间存在强任务间协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的新视角合成（NVS）与开放词汇分割（OVS）融合的前馈3D基础模型依赖于静态场景假设，在无约束动态环境中会导致空间特征严重错位。作者旨在弥合这一关键差距，使3D基础模型能够在动态场景中同时获得高保真的新视角合成和一致的语义理解。

Method: SPAR包含两个核心创新：(1) 联合语义-几何编码架构，在潜在空间聚合前显式隔离瞬态动态噪声；(2) 动态区域感知的端到端训练范式，将运动估计与多视角视觉和语义学习结构化耦合，使网络能够内在地解决运动冲突，并从动态输入中提取多视角一致、时间稳定的场景表示。

Result: 在D-RE10K基准上取得SOTA性能。仅给定3个和4个输入视角时，PSNR分别达到22.15 dB和23.33 dB；尽管以自监督方式训练，运动掩码预测的mIoU达到88.5%。此外，实验分析表明光度场景重建与语义理解之间存在强任务间协同，语义合成学习能持续增强新视角渲染的光度保真度。

Conclusion: SPAR成功地将NVS与OVS统一于动态场景中，通过显式动态噪声隔离和动态区域感知的联合训练，实现了静态场景假设的突破。实验结果验证了该方法在动态挑战场景下的有效性，并揭示了语义-光度任务间的正向协同关系。代码已开源。

Abstract: The integration of novel view synthesis (NVS) and open-vocabulary segmentation (OVS) has recently yielded powerful feed-forward 3D foundation models. However, their inherent reliance on static-scene assumptions leads to severe misalignment of spatial features in unconstrained dynamic environments. To bridge this critical gap, we propose SPAR, a novel joint semantic-geometric encoding architecture that explicitly isolates transient dynamic noise prior to latent space aggregation. Furthermore, we introduce a dynamic-region-aware end-to-end training paradigm that structurally couples motion estimation with multi-view visual and semantic learning. This unified approach enables the network to inherently resolve motion conflicts and distill multi-view consistent, temporally stable scene representations from dynamic inputs. Extensive experiments on the challenging D-RE10K benchmark demonstrate that SPAR achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our end-to-end approach achieves exceptional novel view synthesis quality, yielding a PSNR of 22.15 dB and 23.33 dB given only 3 and 4 input views respectively. Despite being trained in a self-supervised manner, our model achieves an mIoU of 88.5% for motion mask prediction. Furthermore, our analysis reveals a strong inter-task synergy between photometric scene reconstruction and semantic understanding, where semantic synthesis learning consistently enhances photometric fidelity in novel view rendering. Code will be available at https://github.com/dmucby/SPAR.

</details>


### [71] [Foundational feature fusion for conditional flow matching in 6D pose estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29183)
*Amir Hamza,Davide Boscaini,Fabio Poiesi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FunFlow6D 是一种基于条件流匹配的物体6D姿态估计方法，利用几何和外观基础模型的预训练特征，通过交叉注意力融合机制为流匹配模块提供更丰富的条件信息，无需训练任务特定的编码器。在BOP基准的四个数据集上超越现有最先进方法，同时降低监督需求与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件流匹配的6D姿态估计方法需要训练任务特定的编码器（监督于物体-场景重叠），且依赖简单的特征融合策略来处理姿态模糊性，这增加了训练复杂度和资源开销。

Method: 1) 利用几何与外观基础模型的预训练特征进行流匹配姿态估计，避免任务特定编码器训练；2) 设计基于交叉注意力的融合机制，动态结合几何和外观特征，为流匹配模块提供更丰富的条件信息。

Result: 在BOP基准的四个数据集上，FunFlow6D优于先前最先进方法，同时减少了监督需求和内存开销；大量消融实验验证了各提出组件的贡献。

Conclusion: FunFlow6D表明利用基础模型特征与交叉注意力融合可以替代任务特定编码器和简单特征融合，在提升姿态估计精度的同时降低训练成本，为6D姿态估计提供了更高效的新范式。

Abstract: Conditional flow matching has enabled a step forward in object 6D pose estimation, achieving state-of-the-art performance by progressively denoising and registering object representations to observed scenes. Existing methods require training task-specific encoders supervised on object-scene overlap and rely on trivial feature fusion strategies to resolve pose ambiguities. We present FunFlow6D, a novel flow matching-based formulation that leverages features from geometric and appearance foundation models for pose estimation, eliminating the need for task-specific encoder training. We also introduce a cross attention-based fusion mechanism that dynamically combines geometric and appearance features to provide richer conditioning for the flow matching module. Experiments on four datasets from the BOP benchmark show that FunFlow6D outperforms the previous state of the art while reducing supervision requirements and memory overhead. Extensive ablations validate the contribution of each proposed component. Project website: https://tev-fbk.github.io/FunFlow6D/.

</details>


### [72] [Multi-Scale Temporal Domain Alignment for Federated Video Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29186)
*Lee En-Yi Hannah,Haozhi Cao,Yuecong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出METAL框架，通过多尺度时间域对齐解决联邦视频域自适应中的时间信息对齐问题，在Epic-Kitchens-55和Daily-DA数据集上实现最先进性能，最高超过现有FDA方法28.47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦视频域自适应（FVDA）允许在分布式且非IID的视频数据集上进行协作学习同时保护隐私，但由于时间信息对齐的挑战而研究不足。现有方法未能有效利用多尺度时间信息进行跨域视频动作识别。

Method: METAL框架：1）在源客户端训练逐尺度Transformer编码器；2）在每个时间尺度上执行独立知识投票以在目标服务器上生成鲁棒伪标签；3）提出新颖的L2方差惩罚项以在基于尺度的知识蒸馏中强制跨尺度一致性，防止单一主导尺度；4）通过知识蒸馏训练晚期融合头，使用置信度加权的尺度级预测聚合，以利用互补的时间信息进行最终预测。

Result: 在Epic-Kitchens-55和Daily-DA数据集上实现最先进性能，最高提升达28.47%（相对于现有FDA方法）。消融研究表明多尺度蒸馏和尺度协同对于有效的时间知识迁移至关重要。

Conclusion: 多尺度时间域对齐是解决FVDA中时间信息对齐问题的关键，METAL通过尺度协同和知识蒸馏有效提升跨域视频动作识别性能。

Abstract: Federated Video Domain Adaptation (FVDA) enables collaborative learning across distributed and non-IID video datasets while preserving privacy, but is under-explored due to challenges in aligning temporal information. We propose Multi-scalE Temporal domAin aLignment (METAL), a novel framework that leverages temporal information at multiple resolutions to improve cross-domain video action recognition with only model parameter transfers. METAL trains per-scale transformer encoders on source-clients, then performs independent knowledge voting at each temporal scale to generate robust pseudo-labels on the target-server. A novel $L_2$ variance penalty enforces cross-scale consistency during scale-based knowledge distillation, preventing a singular dominant scale. The late fusion aggregates features across different scales, where the fusion head is trained via knowledge distillation using confidence-weighted aggregation of scale-wise predictions, enabling the model to effectively exploit complementary temporal information for final predictions. Experiments on Epic-Kitchens-55 and Daily-DA demonstrate state-of-the-art performances, with gains up to 28.47% over current FDA methods. Ablation studies prove that multi-scale distillation and scale coordination are critical for effective temporal knowledge transfer.

</details>


### [73] [OPUS-V2: Bridging the Gap between Sparse Points and Dense Voxels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29187)
*Jiabao Wang,Qiang Meng,Liujiang Yan,Ke Wang,Qibin Hou,Ming-Ming Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OPUS-V2，一种基于稀疏点集的占据预测（occupancy prediction）框架，通过轻量级的点-体素变换（PVT）模块将稀疏预测自适应映射到稠密体素空间，消除了传统方法中手工设计启发式操作的限制，并在解耦特征与占据生成过程后支持任意占据分辨率。在Occ3D数据集上达到44.0 rayIoU的SOTA性能，在OpenOccupancy数据集上取得16.4 mIoU并实现20.6 FPS的实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于点的占据预测范式虽在精度与效率间取得了良好平衡，但其稀疏预测结果与自动驾驶系统所需的稠密体素占据表征本质不匹配，导致训练和推理阶段需要手工设计的启发式操作（heuristics），进而限制了最终性能。

Method: OPUS-V2在OPUS（基于稀疏集）框架基础上，在解码器后引入轻量级点-体素变换（PVT）模块，自适应地将稀疏预测映射到稠密体素空间，避免次优操作；同时将特征生成与占据生成过程解耦，使框架能够适配任意占据分辨率。

Result: 在Occ3D数据集上取得44.0 rayIoU的最先进（SOTA）结果；在更具挑战性的OpenOccupancy数据集上获得16.4 mIoU，同时以20.6 FPS实现实时运行。

Conclusion: OPUS-V2通过点-体素变换模块消除了稀疏预测与稠密占据表征之间的不匹配问题，无需手工启发式操作即提升了模型精度，并凭借特征与占据生成的解耦实现了对任意分辨率的灵活适配，在公开数据集上证明了其精度与效率的兼顾能力。

Abstract: The point-based occupancy prediction paradigm has achieved an attractive trade-off between accuracy and efficiency by modeling 3D space sparsely. However, its predictions inherently mismatch the dense voxel-based occupancy required by self-driving systems, necessitating hand-crafted heuristics during training and inference that limit final performance. To overcome these limitations, we propose OPUS-V2, a novel framework built upon the pioneering OPUS (occupancy prediction using a sparse set) point-based approach. OPUS-V2 incorporates a lightweight point-voxel transformation (PVT) module behind the decoder to adaptively map sparse predictions into the dense voxel space, eliminating the need for suboptimal operations and improving model accuracy. Furthermore, our architecture decouples feature and occupancy generation processes, allowing OPUS-V2 to adapt to arbitrary occupancy resolutions. OPUS-V2 achieves a state-of-the-art rayIoU of 44.0 on the Occ3D dataset. On the more challenging OpenOccupancy dataset, it attains a competitive 16.4 mIoU while running in real time at 20.6 FPS.

</details>


### [74] [RLG-TPV: Radar- and LiDAR-Guided Tri-Perspective View Fusion for Camera-Radar 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29194)
*Ahmet Mete Dokgoz,A. Enes Doruk,Hasan F. Ates*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RLG-TPV，一个基于三视角（TPV）表示的相机-雷达多模态3D目标检测框架。通过雷达和训练时的LiDAR提供几何引导，弥补纯相机TPV提昇中沿投影射线深度歧义的问题。采用射线引导的可变形注意力提昇、LiDAR监督的类别占用训练侧/前平面（推理时移除）、Doppler引导的时间融合及RCS感知的雷达散点处理，在nuScenes上达到0.4981 mAP和0.5959 NDS，相比CRN基线将方向与速度误差分别降低31.9%和30.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TPV（三视角）表征通过顶、侧、前三个特征平面描述3D场景结构，但TPV提昇主要依赖相机，导致沿投影射线采样的图像证据深度存在歧义。作者希望引入雷达（及训练时的LiDAR）提供互补的几何引导，以解决纯相机TPV提昇中深度模糊的问题。

Method: （1）射线引导的可变形注意力提昇：用LiDAR监督的相机深度概率与雷达视锥占用加权采样图像特征，雷达进一步在提昇前细化深度分布；（2）训练策略：利用LiDAR导出的类别占用目标监督侧、前平面训练，推理时移除对应head，部署仅需相机和雷达；（3）时间融合：Doppler引导的时间融合，以雷达径向速度场锚定运动估计并对齐历史特征，并通过门控限制无支撑运动区域的扭曲；（4）RCS感知的雷达散点：基于雷达截面积（RCS）条件化地让雷达证据在空间邻域内传播。

Result: 在nuScenes验证集上，RLG-TPV达到0.4981 mAP和0.5959 NDS，相对已发表的CRN基线将方向误差降低31.9%、速度误差降低30.7%。消融实验表明射线级几何引导是最终性能的主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: 通过多模态几何引导（雷达+训练时LiDAR）有效解决了纯相机TPV提昇的深度歧义问题，在不增加部署传感器负担（推理仅需相机和雷达）的前提下显著提升3D检测精度，证明射线级几何引导是该框架性能提升的核心驱动。

Abstract: Tri-Perspective View (TPV) representations describe 3D scene structure through top, side, and front feature planes, but existing TPV lifting is primarily camera-based, leaving the depth of sampled image evidence ambiguous along projected camera rays. We propose RLG-TPV, a multimodal TPV framework for camera-radar 3D object detection in which radar and training-time LiDAR provide complementary geometric guidance during representation construction. A ray-guided deformable-attention lift weights sampled image features using LiDAR-supervised camera depth probabilities and radar frustum occupancy, while radar additionally refines the depth distribution before lifting. Because conventional radar provides limited elevation information, LiDAR-derived class-occupancy targets supervise the side and front planes during training; the corresponding heads are removed at inference, so deployment requires only cameras and radar. For temporal aggregation, Doppler-guided temporal fusion aligns past features using a motion field anchored by measured radar radial velocity, with gating that limits warping in regions without supported motion. An RCS-aware radar scatter further allows radar evidence to spread over spatial neighborhoods conditioned on radar cross section. On the nuScenes validation set, RLG-TPV achieves 0.4981 mAP and 0.5959 NDS, reducing orientation and velocity error by 31.9\% and 30.7\% relative to the published CRN baseline. Ablation studies show that ray-level geometric guidance is a major contributor to the final performance.

</details>


### [75] [Bayesian-Optimized Superpixel-GrabCut for Traceable Optic Disc Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29196)
*Shraddha Changune,Vivek Noel Soren,Gautam Das,Tapan Kumar Gandhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全算法可追踪且可训练的视盘分割流水线，结合超像素分解、混合亮度-邻近性评分、形态学正则化、迭代GrabCut精炼和椭圆拟合，通过贝叶斯优化自动调参，在Drishti-GS数据集上达到0.9536的Dice系数，匹敌当前最先进水平，同时提供透明、确定性的黑盒替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在视盘分割中表现优秀但缺乏推理阶段的可计算追踪性，这在临床工作流中对算法审计和失败分析至关重要。

Method: 提出一个完全算法可追踪的分割流水线，依次结合超像素分解、混合亮度-邻近性超像素评分、形态学正则化、迭代GrabCut精炼和椭圆形状拟合；将超参数优化建模为目标函数并用贝叶斯优化自动求解，消除手动调参。

Result: 在Drishti-GS数据集上达到0.9536的Dice系数，匹敌现有最先进方法的性能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过在各处理阶段保持明确的数学透明度，为医学审查和调试提供了确定性、可追踪的替代黑盒架构的方案。

Abstract: Optic disc (OD) segmentation is essential for diagnosing ophthalmic pathologies from retinal fundus images. However, prevailing deep learning approaches operate as opaque black boxes, lacking the inference-stage mathematical traceability--a critical requirement for algorithmic auditing and failure analysis in clinical workflows. This paper presents a fully algorithmically traceable and trainable segmentation pipeline that jointly combines superpixel decomposition, hybrid brightness-proximity superpixel scoring, morphological regularization, iterative GrabCut refinement, and elliptical shape fitting. The hyperparameter optimization is formulated as an objective function and solved via Bayesian optimization to eliminate manual parameter tuning. A quantitative evaluation on the Drishti-GS dataset demonstrates that our method achieves a Dice coefficient of 0.9536, matching state-of-the-art performance. By maintaining explicit mathematical transparency across all processing stages, our framework offers a deterministic, traceable alternative to black-box architectures for medical review and debugging.

</details>


### [76] [Ground-to-Satellite Localization in Unconstrained Image Collections for 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29211)
*Angel Daruna,Ben Southall,Niluthpol Chowdhury Mithun,Kshitij Minhas,Nicholas Meegan,Qiao Wang,Bogdan Matei,Supun Samarasekera,Rakesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的层次化跨视角定位框架，利用从无约束地面图像集合中推导出的运动恢复结构（SfM）模型的几何约束，实现地面图像与卫星影像的定位对齐。该方法通过跨视角匹配生成由粗到细的位姿假设，并利用核密度估计聚合SfM模型中的噪声预测以筛选离群值。实验表明该方法在挑战性图像集合上定位可靠，且卫星参考对齐支持精确的度量尺度估计、双胞胎检测（doppelgänger detection）以及不连通SfM重建的合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角定位方法需要全景图像或已知初始位置等严格条件，限制了其在野外（in-the-wild）无约束图像重建场景中的适用性。作者旨在建立一种无需上述严格前提、可直接利用无约束地面图像集合的跨视角定位框架，以支持度量精确的地理定位3D场景重建。

Method: 提出一种鲁棒的层次化跨视角定位框架：首先从无约束地面图像集合重建SfM模型，利用其几何约束；然后通过跨视角匹配方法生成由粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的位姿假设；接着使用核密度估计（Kernel Density Estimation）跨SfM模型聚合噪声预测，恢复一致性对齐并过滤离群值。

Result: 在具有挑战性的图像集合上验证了可靠的定位性能。实验进一步表明卫星参考对齐能够实现精确的度量尺度估计、双胞胎检测以及不连通SfM重建的合并，从而生成比单纯SfM更完整、更地理定位化的站点模型。

Conclusion: 卫星影像参考下的跨视角定位不仅解决了无约束图像集合的粗定位问题，还显著提升了SfM重建的质量和完整性，为大规模野外3D场景重建提供了一种可行且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Ground image localization with respect to satellite imagery is a key enabler for metrically-accurate, geo-localized 3D scene reconstruction from unconstrained image collections. Existing cross-view localization methods have strict requirements such as panoramic imagery or known initial locations, limiting their applicability for in-the-wild reconstruction settings. We propose a robust hierarchical cross-view localization framework that leverages geometric constraints from Structure-from-Motion (SfM) models derived from unconstrained ground image collections. Our method generates coarse-to-fine pose hypotheses through a cross-view matching approach and aggregates noisy predictions across SfM model(s) using Kernel Density Estimation to recover consensus alignments while filtering outliers. Experiments demonstrate reliable localization performance from challenging image collections. Empirically we found satellite-referenced alignment enables accurate metric scale estimation, doppelgänger detection, and merging of disjoint SfM reconstructions, resulting in more complete, geo-localized site models than are possible with SfM alone.

</details>


### [77] [SGPDFuse: Semantically-Guided Physics-Disentanglement General Multi-Modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29220)
*Haozhen Wei,Chengjun Jiang,Yutong Guo,Xinrui Ju,Xingyuan Li,Xiang Chen,Jinyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGPDFuse，一种基于物理解耦的多模态图像融合方法。通过语义-物理参数桥（SPPB）将输入映射为物理解耦的结构化表示，利用Intrinsic-Variation原则分离不变场景属性与瞬态环境因素，并通过语义对齐机制（余弦相似度锚定显著语义特征+Gram矩阵正则化约束物理纹理）引导分解，在红外-可见光、多聚焦、多曝光基准上以单一架构取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMIF方法依赖盲特征聚合，擅长信号累加但无法区分关键内容与物理退化，导致融合结果难以保留真实场景本质并消除环境干扰。核心问题在于缺乏对物理语义的解耦能力。

Method: 1) 提出语义-物理参数桥（SPPB），基于预训练视觉基础模型将输入映射为物理解耦的结构化表示；2) 利用Intrinsic-Variation原则解耦不变场景属性与瞬态环境因素；3) 引入语义对齐机制：通过余弦相似度将融合表示锚定到基础模型特征空间中的显著语义特征以保留关键目标，同时用Gram矩阵正则化保证物理纹理保真度，消除非自然伪影。

Result: 在红外-可见光、多聚焦和多曝光三种基准任务上，使用单一架构均达到state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: SGPDFuse通过物理解耦的语义表示替换盲特征聚合，验证了基于预训练基础模型+物理先验引导的解耦范式在多模态图像融合中的有效性，能够在保留关键语义目标的同时严格消除环境退化伪影。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion (MMIF) aims to integrate complementary sensor data into a single representation that preserves intrinsic scene reality while eliminating environmental interferences. Most existing approaches rely on blind feature aggregation, which excels at signal accumulation but fails to distinguish essential content from physical degradations. We propose SGPDFuse, which bridges this gap by mapping inputs into a physics-disentangled structural representation via a Semantic-Physical Parametric Bridge (SPPB) built on pretrained vision foundation models, utilizing the Intrinsic-Variation principle to decouple invariant scene attributes from transient environmental factors. To guide this decomposition, we introduce a Semantic Alignment mechanism: we explicitly anchor the fused representation to salient semantic features in the same foundation model feature space via cosine similarity to preserve critical targets, while enforcing physical texture fidelity through Gram-matrix regularization to strictly eliminate unnatural artifacts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SGPDFuse achieves state-of-the-art performance across infrared-visible, multi-focus, and multi-exposure benchmarks using a single architecture.

</details>


### [78] [Using Channel Representations in Regularization Terms: A Case Study on Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29227)
*Christian Heinemann,Freddie Åström,George Baravdish,Kai Krajsek,Michael Felsberg,Hanno Scharr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于通道表示的非线性扩散滤波方法，通过软直方图能量泛函推导出带加权项的鲁棒扩散更新方案，在图像重建与去噪任务中表现优异，尤其在混合高斯噪声和脉冲噪声（如缺失数据）场景下具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性扩散滤波方法在处理混合噪声类型（如高斯噪声与脉冲噪声共存）时性能有限，需要一种能够同时适应多种噪声模型的鲁棒扩散机制。

Method: 基于通道编码构建图像像素邻域的软直方图表示，据此提出新的能量泛函；通过求解Euler-Lagrange方程得到带加权项的非线性鲁棒扩散更新方案，权重项来自通道表示并引导扩散过程。

Result: 在图像重建问题中，面对高斯噪声与脉冲噪声（如缺失数据）混合的场景表现出良好性能；在标量图像去噪实验中，与现有扩散方案及最新去噪方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于通道表示的软直方图能量泛函能够有效指导非线性扩散滤波，为混合噪声下的图像重建和去噪提供了一种有竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work we propose a novel non-linear diffusion filtering approach for images based on their channel representation. To derive the diffusion update scheme we formulate a novel energy functional using a soft-histogram representation of image pixel neighborhoods obtained from the channel encoding. The resulting Euler-Lagrange equation yields a non-linear robust diffusion scheme with additional weighting terms stemming from the channel representation which steer the diffusion process. We apply this novel energy formulation to image reconstruction problems, showing good performance in the presence of mixtures of Gaussian and impulse-like noise, e.g. missing data. In denoising experiments of common scalar-valued images our approach performs competitive compared to other diffusion schemes as well as state-of-the-art denoising methods for the considered noise types.

</details>

### [79] [NLP-Driven Knowledge Extraction and Thematic Classification of Translated Ancient Indian Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28608)
*M. S. Rajeevan,B. Mini Devi,V. S. Anoop,C. Mallikarjuna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用自然语言处理技术（包括命名实体识别、BERTopic主题建模和Neo4j知识图谱）对古代印度医学文献《妙闻集》（Sushruta Samhita）的英文译本进行结构化处理，实现医学概念提取、分类与可视化，从而弥合传统文献与现代数据驱动研究之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古代印度医学文本（如《妙闻集》）蕴含丰富的疾病、治疗和外科手术知识，但其古老格式和复杂词汇导致可访问性和系统性整理存在困难，亟需计算化手段实现数字化的知识组织与利用。

Method: 本研究综合运用三类NLP方法：(1) 命名实体识别（NER）用于结构化识别疾病、治疗、研究者和药用植物等实体；(2) BERTopic主题建模用于识别底层医学主题并进行主题分类；(3) 基于Neo4j的知识图谱开发实现抽取实体间关系的语义网络表示，支持知识检索与数字保存。

Result: 研究结果表明，图数据库、主题建模与实体识别相结合能够有效实现Ayurveda历史医学知识的计算化组织，成功缩小了传统文献与当代数据驱动研究之间的差距，并为历史文本分析、医学信息学和数字人文领域提供了技术支撑。

Conclusion: 该研究方法促进了古代印度医学智慧的可访问性与可理解性，为传统文本的计算化处理与数字人文研究提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Ancient Indian medical texts like Sushruta Samhita have extensive information on diseases, treatments, and surgical techniques. Yet, their ancient format and use of intricate vocabulary pose difficulties in accessibility and systematic ordering. The research here utilizes Natural Language Processing (NLP) methods like Named Entity Recognition (NER), BERTopic modeling, and Knowledge Graph development in Neo4j to extract, categorize, and visualize important concepts based on translated versions. Thematic classification with BERTopic allows for the identification of the underlying medical topics, whereas NER supports the structured entity recognition of diseases, treatments, researchers, and medicinal plants. Graphbased network analysis with Neo4j also allows for the semantic representation of relationship among extracted entities, supporting knowledge retrieval and digital preservation. The findings illustrate how graph databases, topic modeling, and entity recognition facilitate the computational organization of Ayurveda's historical medical wisdom, closing the gap between the conventional texts and contemporary data-driven inquiry. The suggested method promotes historical text analysis, medical informatics, and digital humanities to make ancient Indian medical wisdom more accessible and understandable.

</details>


### [80] [Parametric Multimodal User Memory: Storing What Captions Cannot Carry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28609)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将个性化代理的感知记忆（如声音、人脸等不可言说的身份特征）直接嵌入模型，通过VLM定位指代对象（what/where）与专用编码器提取身份键（who）的分解方案，以单一内联token存储身份并在生成时通过注意力读取，无需外部检索。相比纯文本记忆，该方法在五个模态上将身份识别召回率从0.11提升至接近oracle水平（0.96），并在12个域、1080个任务的PerceptMem基准上验证了"感知身份容量受限、事实文本绑定受限"的结论，两者组合可同时记住用户说了什么以及用户是什么样的人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化代理的用户记忆几乎全部依赖文本（转录和按相似度检索的标题），只能捕捉可命名的一半信息，而无法保存声音、人脸随年龄和光照的变化、语调等感知信息。论文量化了这一损失：强大的基于标题的重识别器在五个模态上仅能恢复专用编码器0.11的召回率，在不可命名信号上趋于随机水平。

Method: 将感知记忆ground到模型中，把回忆分解为两个子问题：(1) 视觉-语言模型在上下文中定位指代对象（what和where）；(2) 专用编码器提取身份键（who），存储为一个内联token，在生成时通过注意力直接读取，无需外部往返检索。识别核心是免训练的，可在任意冻结模型上以O(1)注册成本复现编码器的召回能力。

Result: 单独使用VLM跨年龄人脸识别仅达0.54召回率（人脸编码器为0.81），无grounding的编码器在双人场景中指代识别仅0.05；两者结合达到正确区域oracle水平0.96，并泛化到多说话人音频和视频。在PerceptMem基准（12个域、1,080个任务）上，感知身份呈容量受限，而确切事实呈绑定受限。

Conclusion: 感知身份应存储在参数化记忆库中，确切事实应存储在文本存储中，两者可干净组合。拥有两种记忆的代理不仅能记住用户说了什么，还能记住用户是什么样的人，弥补了纯文本记忆在感知维度上的根本缺失。

Abstract: A personalized agent needs a user memory: a persistent model of who its user is. Today it is almost always text -- transcripts and captions retrieved by similarity. This serves the captionable half of a person ("my cat is named Bibi"), but discards the perceptual half no caption can hold: how a voice sounds, how a face reads across age and lighting, how tired someone sounds. We measure this loss across five modalities: a strong caption-based re-identifier recovers as little as 0.11 of a dedicated encoder's recall, collapsing toward chance on non-nameable signals.
  We instead ground perceptual memory in the model, decomposing recall into two subproblems: a vision-language model grounds the referent in context (what and where), and a dedicated encoder extracts an identity key (who), stored as one inline token read by attention at generation with no external round-trip. Neither suffices alone -- the VLM identifies cross-age faces at only 0.54 recall where a face encoder reaches 0.81, and an ungrounded encoder recognizes a two-person-scene referent at 0.05 -- yet together they reach correct-region oracle (0.96), generalizing to multi-speaker audio and video. The recognition core is training-free: it reproduces the encoder's recall on any frozen model at O(1) registration cost. On PerceptMem (12 domains, 1,080 tasks) perceptual identity is capacity-limited while exact facts are binding-limited: identity belongs in a parametric bank, facts in a text store. The two memories compose cleanly: an agent with both can remember not only what its user said, but also what they are like.

</details>


### [81] [Gurukul AI: An Interactive AI-Driven Educational Platform for Indian Education System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28611)
*Isha Narang,Sneh Gosai,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GurukulAI，一个面向印度NCERT课程体系的AI教育平台。作者构建了基于NCERT教材（9-12年级、5门学科）的18,720对问答数据集，基于该数据集微调LLaMA 3.1 8B模型，并部署在检索增强生成（RAG）框架中，支持英语和印地语双语交互，旨在弥合通用大语言模型与印度区域性教育需求之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（如ChatGPT、LLaMA）主要基于西方中心数据训练，难以适配印度这样语言多样化、考试导向、依赖标准化大纲的区域性教育体系。目前缺乏与印度教学大纲对齐的数据集和工具。

Method: 1) 基于NCERT教材（9-12年级）构建与教学大纲对齐的QA数据集，涵盖5门学科，共18,720个问答对；2) 使用该数据集对LLaMA 3.1 8B模型进行微调；3) 将微调模型部署在面向教育场景定制的检索增强生成（RAG）框架中；4) 构建GurukulAI开放平台，支持英语和印地语双语交互及考试风格练习。

Result: 生成了包含18,720个问答对、覆盖5门学科的NCERT对齐数据集，构建了可用的GurukulAI双语开放平台，数据与代码均已公开（HuggingFace和GitHub）。

Conclusion: 通过将通用LLM能力本地化适配印度课堂，该工作有效弥合了通用大模型能力与区域性教育需求之间的鸿沟，为大语言模型在教育场景中的区域化应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) like ChatGPT and LLaMA have transformed AI-driven education, but these systems are predominantly trained on Western-centric data, making them ill-suited for regional curricula like India's. The Indian education system is linguistically diverse, exam-oriented, and structured around standardized syllabi, not addressed by existing datasets or tools. In this work, we curate a syllabus-aligned QA dataset based on NCERT (National Council of Educational Research and Training) textbooks for classes 9-12, capturing the content, context, and teaching style of Indian curricula. The final dataset, comprising 18,720 question-answer pairs across five subjects, is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/LingoIITGN/Gurukul. We fine-tune the LLaMA 3.1 8B model using this dataset and deploy it in a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework tailored to educational needs. We introduce GurukulAI, an open-access platform that enables Indian students to chat with the model, get doubts cleared, practice exam-style questions, receive contextual answers, and interact in both English and Hindi. By localizing AI for Indian classrooms, our work bridges the gap between global LLM capabilities and regional educational demands. The code is available at https://github.com/lingo-iitgn/GurukulAI.

</details>


### [82] [STAGEET: Stage-wise Typed Edit Tagging for Grammatical Error Correction with Arabic as a Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28614)
*Wenjie Lou,Alaa Mamdouh Akef*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出STAGEET，一种阶段式类型化编辑标注框架，将序列到编辑（Seq2Edit）的监督信号重组为带类型的可执行阶段，每个阶段预测自己的标签空间并逐步重写当前假设，实现了可检查的修正轨迹，同时在QALB-2014上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Seq2Edit方法的可解释性主要是操作层面的——标签仅说明字符串如何改变，但不一定揭示修正的类型（如拼写、语法、词序等）。单一编辑词汇表无法显式呈现修正类别，缺乏可检查的修正过程。

Method: STAGEET将语法纠错分解为有序的中粒度类型化阶段序列；每个阶段拥有独立的标签空间，对当前假设执行一次重写，并将中间结果传递给下一阶段。框架实例化为两种变体：(1) 端到端共享编码器多头模型，配阶段特定适配器；(2) 完全专门化变体，每个阶段使用独立的标注器。

Result: 在QALB-2014和ZAEBUC数据集上的实验表明，类别感知的阶段式修正保持了与编辑型GEC相当的竞争力，同时提供了更可检查的修正轨迹；并在QALB-2014上取得了最新的最优结果（SOTA）。

Conclusion: STAGEET证明可以通过将编辑操作扩展为修正类别并组织为类型化阶段，在不牺牲GEC性能的前提下提升编辑型方法的可检查性，为面向可解释性的GEC提供了新思路。

Abstract: Sequence-to-edit approaches make grammatical error correction (GEC) efficient and locally interpretable by predicting edit labels over the input rather than generating a full corrected sentence. Their interpretability, however, is primarily operational: a label specifies how the string should change, but a single edit vocabulary does not always reveal the type of correction being made. We propose STAGEET, a stage-wise typed edit-tagging framework that reorganizes Seq2Edit supervision into typed executable stages and extends edit operations to correction categories. STAGEET decomposes correction into an ordered sequence of medium-grained typed stages; each stage predicts from its own label space, rewrites the current hypothesis once, and passes the resulting intermediate sentence to the next stage. We instantiate the framework as both an end-to-end shared-encoder multi-head model with stage-specific adapters and a fully specialized variant with one independent tagger per stage. Experiments on QALB-2014 and ZAEBUC show that category-aware staged correction retains competitive edit-based GEC performance while exposing a more inspectable correction trajectory, and attains state-of-the-art results on QALB-2014.

</details>


### [83] [From GenAI Virtual Patient Dialogue Logs to Teacher-Interpretable Process Evidence: A Learning Analytics Study in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28619)
*Xinyu Li,Zijian Li,Mengyu Xia,Luzhen Tang,Naping Chen,Changmin Lin,Danijela Gasevic,Dragan Gasevic,Yizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对1030段生成式AI虚拟病人（GenAI VP）问诊对话进行编码分析，旨在将完整对话日志转化为教师可解读的临床推理过程证据。通过对210名二年级医学生五周胸痛病例的对话进行行为频率、认知网络分析（ENA）和转换网络分析（TNA）三层分析，发现高评分问诊不仅活动量更大，更体现在信息收集与症状探索同沟通、核查、组织和综合行为的联结上，以及总结组织行为更多导向验证和机制性追问。研究证明分层分析GenAI VP对话日志可揭示与高评分问诊相关的过程模式，支持医学教育中的过程导向反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI虚拟病人使问诊练习可大规模重复并保留逐轮对话日志，但这些日志在教学中难以直接使用：完整转录过于繁琐不适合教师常规审查，而最终分数又无法反映学习者是否跟进病人线索、核查不确定性或利用总结引导后续提问。因此需要一种方法将对话过程转化为教师可解读的临床推理过程证据。

Method: 对1030段来自210名二年级医学生的五周胸痛病例GenAI VP对话进行教师评分（使用评估完整问诊对话的量规），按周中位数将咨询分为高/低评分两类。对同一编码对话数据应用三层分析方法：行为频率分析（行为盛行度）、局部共现分析（认知网络分析ENA）、顺序转换分析（转换网络分析TNA）。

Result: 高评分咨询涉及更多问诊行为，但差异不仅体现在数量上。高评分咨询更频繁地将信息收集与症状探索同沟通、核查、组织和综合行为相联结；总结和组织行为更常导向验证或机制导向的追问。

Conclusion: 对GenAI VP对话日志的分层分析能够揭示与高评分问诊过程相关的模式，为医学教育提供过程性反馈支持，使大规模AI虚拟病人对话日志可用于教学评估。

Abstract: Medical history taking is a dialogue-based clinical reasoning task in which learners must gather, organise, and integrate patient information while the consultation unfolds. Generative AI-powered virtual patients (GenAI VPs) make repeated history taking practice scalable and preserve full turn by turn dialogue. However, these logs are educationally difficult to use directly. Complete transcripts are too detailed for routine teacher review, whereas final scores obscure whether learners followed up patient cues, checked uncertainty, or used summaries to guide later questioning. This study examined whether coded GenAI VP dialogues can provide teacher-interpretable process evidence of clinical reasoning. We analysed 1{,}030 GenAI VP dialogues from 210 second-year medical learners across five weeks chest-pain cases. Each consultation was teacher-scored using a rubric assessing the full history taking dialogue, and consultations were classified within each week as high- or low-rated using the weekly median score. To explain how rated performance was reflected in the dialogue process, we applied three analytic layers to the same coded dialogue data: behavioural prevalence, local co-occurrence using Epistemic Network Analysis, and sequential transition using Transition Network Analysis. High-rated consultations involved more history taking activity, but differences were not simply about volume. High rated consultations more often connected information gathering and symptom exploration with communication, checking, organisation, and synthesis. Summarising and organising moves more often led to verification or mechanism-oriented follow-up. These findings show how layered analysis of GenAI VP dialogue logs can reveal process patterns associated with high rated history taking and support process-focused feedback in medical education.

</details>


### [84] [Looking Again: Measuring Sycophancy in the Reasoning Chains of Multimodal Models Under Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28623)
*Mahir Numayeer Islam,Gakuto Okuyama,Nikolaus Siauw,Shivank Garg,Madhur Panwar,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于评估大型多模态推理模型（LMRM）谄媚性（sycophancy）的基准和数据集，该基准将四个视觉化推理数据集与五种压力条件配对，在单轮和多轮设置下同时评估最终答案层面和推理链层面的谄媚行为。研究发现谄媚行为在压力条件下普遍存在，且推理链层面的谄媚可独立于最终答案破坏推理过程，仅评估答案层面是不够的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态推理模型的能力日益增强，主要通过生成显式思维链推理来实现。在语言模型中已观察到这种性能往往伴随谄媚性（模型倾向于同意用户而非依据证据），但目前尚无可靠方法评估LMRM的谄媚性。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立针对LMRM的谄媚性评测基准。

Method: 构建了一个基准和数据集，将四个基于视觉的数据集（涵盖数学、临床、时间、人口推理）与五种压力条件（Statement压力最高、Conviction最低等）配对，在单轮和多轮设置下评估。不仅评估最终答案中的谄媚性，还评估思维链中谄媚性的出现。提出了将推理链层面与答案层面谄媚性区分的失败分类法，以及定位推理链中漂移首次出现的句子级分类法。

Result: 谄媚性在压力条件下普遍存在；Statement压力引发最高谄媚率，Conviction最低（除Mistral-Small-4外）。在多轮压力下，临床视觉判断的推理链层面谄媚性急剧加剧，受影响最严重的模型达到95.7%。推理链谄媚可独立于最终答案破坏推理过程，仅评估答案层面不足。

Conclusion: LMRM的谄媚问题需要在推理链层面进行评估而不仅限于最终答案，本文提供的基准、数据集及分类法为这一方向的系统性研究奠定了基础，表明谄媚性可独立于答案层面在推理过程中产生支配性影响。

Abstract: Large multimodal reasoning models (LMRMs) are getting increasingly capable, primarily through generating explicit chain-of-thought reasoning before answering. In language models it has been observed that this performance often comes with sycophancy, the tendency of a model to agree with the user over the evidence. However, for LMRMs no reliable method to measure sycophancy yet exists. We bridge this gap by introducing a benchmark and dataset for evaluating LMRM sycophancy when confronted with a wrong answer from a user. Our benchmark pairs four visually grounded datasets spanning mathematical, clinical, temporal, and demographic reasoning with five pressure conditions in single-turn and multi-turn settings. We evaluate sycophancy in the final answer as well as its emergence within the reasoning chain. We find that sycophancy is prevalent under pressure, with Statement pressure eliciting the highest rates and Conviction the lowest for all models except Mistral-Small-4, and under multi-turn pressure reasoning-level sycophancy intensifies sharply in clinical visual judgement, reaching 95.7% for the most affected model. We further introduce a failure taxonomy separating reasoning-chain from answer-level sycophancy, and a complementary sentence-level taxonomy locating where in the chain drift first emerges. Our results show that sycophancy can corrupt the reasoning chain independently of the final answer, so answer-level evaluation alone is insufficient.

</details>


### [85] [MA-RAG: Multi-Agent Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Query-Driven Summarization of Longitudinal Parkinson's Disease Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28624)
*Sana Alamgeera,Denise Goberta,Muhammad Irshad,Anne H. H. Ngu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-RAG，一个面向帕金森病临床纵向结构化评估数据的查询驱动多智能体检索增强生成框架，通过领域专用智能体分解临床推理、结构化事实提取和最终验证阶段，显著提升事实准确性和时序一致性。在客观指标（Fact Precision从0.436提升至0.990，Hallucination Rate从0.564降至0.010）和临床专家主观评价上均大幅优于传统、RAG-only和单智能体RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病单次就诊和纵向临床评估的准确解读耗时且依赖专科经验。现有LLM虽能生成自然语言摘要，但缺乏领域特定的临床基础，难以对结构化纵向评估数据产生事实正确且时序一致的响应。

Method: 提出MA-RAG框架，采用查询驱动的多智能体检索增强生成架构：将临床推理分解为领域专用智能体，组合结构化事实提取，并通过最终验证阶段合成具有临床基础的摘要。支持四项临床分析任务：单次会诊、纵向轨迹、对比和队列摘要。

Result: MA-RAG相比传统方法、RAG-only和单智能体RAG基线，Fact Precision相对提升最高达122%（0.436→0.990），Hallucination Rate降低最高98%（0.564→0.010），在时序保真度和语义相似度上也表现优异，且临床专家对组织性和临床实用性给予最高评分。

Conclusion: 领域专用多智能体推理能够实现对结构化纵向临床评估数据的可靠查询驱动摘要生成，为临床文档自动化提供了可行且有效的方法。

Abstract: Accurate interpretation of single-visit and longitudinal clinical assessments for Parkinson's disease is time-consuming and often depends on specialist expertise. Although large language models (LLMs) can generate natural language summaries, they frequently lack domain-specific clinical grounding and struggle to produce factually correct and temporally consistent responses for structured longitudinal assessment data. To address these limitations, we propose MA-RAG, a query-driven multi-agent retrieval-augmented generation framework that decomposes clinical reasoning into domain-specialized agents, combines structured fact extraction, and synthesizes clinically grounded summaries through a final verification stage. The framework supports four clinical analysis tasks: single-session, trajectory, comparison, and cohort summarization. We evaluate MA-RAG using objective metrics, namely Fact Precision, Hallucination Rate, Temporal Fidelity, and Semantic Similarity, together with subjective evaluations conducted by clinical experts. Compared to Traditional, RAG-only, and Single-agent RAG baselines, MA-RAG substantially improves factual correctness, achieving up to a 122% relative increase in Fact Precision (from 0.436 to 0.990) and reducing the Hallucination Rate by up to 98% (from 0.564 to 0.010), while consistently receiving top ratings from clinical experts for organization and clinical usefulness. These results demonstrate that domain-specialized multi-agent reasoning enables reliable query-driven summarization of structured longitudinal clinical assessment data.

</details>


### [86] [Asymmetric Within-Document Predictive Learning for Scientific Document Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28625)
*You Zuo,Éric de la Clergerie,Benoît Sagot*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SciJEPA，一种基于论文话语结构、不依赖引用关系的科学文档表示预测预训练框架。它通过非对称的文档内预测（标题/摘要→方法→结论）来学习表示。实验表明，纯预测训练可行但弱于使用相同段落对的对比基线，而加入Sliced各向同性高斯正则化（SIGReg）可显著提升性能并缩小差距，但正则化效果具有任务依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文档表示学习通常依赖引用关系作为监督信号，但引用数据存在稀缺性、噪声和时滞等问题。本文旨在探索一种不依赖引用关系的预训练范式，利用论文自身的结构信息（章节间语义关联）进行表示学习，为科学文档表示提供citation-free的替代或补充方案。

Method: 提出SciJEPA框架，采用非对称的文档内预测学习范式：使用标题和摘要的表示预测方法章节的表示，再用方法章节的表示预测结论章节的表示。同时引入Sliced Isotropic Gaussian Regularization（SIGReg）以约束嵌入几何。实验在RELISH、高影响力引用预测、SciDocs和引用预测任务上与使用相同段落对的受控对比基线进行比较。

Result: 纯预测训练可行但在各任务上弱于受控对比基线；加入SIGReg后性能显著提升并与对比基线差距缩小。正则化效果具有任务依赖性：中等强度的SIGReg有助于细粒度排序任务，更强正则化可能削弱局部对齐。此外，不同编码分支在不同检索场景中各有优势。

Conclusion: 文档内预测学习在精心控制嵌入几何的前提下，可作为科学文档表示学习中一种有前景的citation-free补充方案。嵌入空间几何的调节（如SIGReg）对预测式预训练的成功至关重要，且不同编码分支的异质性可用于适配不同检索需求。

Abstract: We study predictive pretraining for scientific document representation using the discourse structure of papers. We propose SciJEPA, a citation-free framework that learns through asymmetric within-document prediction: title and abstract representations are used to predict method representations, and method representations are used to predict conclusion representations. Experiments on RELISH, high-influence citation, SciDocs, and cite prediction show that plain predictive training is viable but weaker than a controlled contrastive baseline using the same section pairs. Adding Sliced Isotropic Gaussian Regularization (SIGReg) substantially improves performance and narrows this gap. The effect of regularization is task-dependent: moderate SIGReg helps fine-grained ranking, while stronger regularization can weaken local alignment. We further show that different encoding branches support different retrieval regimes. These results position within-document predictive learning as a promising citation-free complement for scientific document representation, provided that embedding geometry is carefully controlled.

</details>


### [87] [Do large language models scrutinise what they review? A multimodal audit of scoring calibration, error detection, and author-identity effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28626)
*Emad Alharbi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了两种多模态大语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Pixtral-Large-124B）作为ICLR 2026同行评审员的表现，覆盖165篇论文。结果显示LLM评分普遍偏高（7.0-8.1），远超人类均分（3.4-6.8）；在错误检测方面，模型仅能检测出12.1%的已验证错误，验证提示可将检出率提升至22.2%，但78%的错误仍未被发现；提供图像反而降低了错误检测率并提高了评分；作者身份（高/低声望机构）不影响评分或错误检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地被用于生成同行评审意见，有必要系统评估其批判性评价能力，特别是识别模型在评分分布、错误检测、多模态信息处理及偏见等方面的表现，以判断其能否可靠地替代人类审稿人。

Method: 选取165篇提交至ICLR 2026（晚于两个模型训练截止日期）的论文，使用Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Pixtral-Large-124B两种多模态LLM进行评审。实验变量包括：作者身份（匿名、高声望机构、低声望机构）、输入格式（纯文本或文本加图像）。同时在55篇论文中人为插入145处可验证的错误，分别使用自然提示和验证导向提示评估模型的错误检测能力。

Result: LLM评分范围为7.0-8.1（包括被拒绝的论文），而人类均分为3.4-6.8；自然提示下错误检出率为12.1%，加入一句验证指示后提升至22.2%，但仍有78%的错误未被检测到；提供图像会降低错误检测但提高评分；没有视觉错误能可靠地对照相应图像得到验证，且一半的纯文本评审描述了并未提供的图像；作者身份对评分和错误检测均无影响；LLM的编辑决定与简单分数平均产生的结果完全一致。

Conclusion: LLM在当前阶段不具备可靠替代人类同行评审员的批判性评价能力，存在评分膨胀、错误检测率低下、多模态处理中的幻觉（描述不存在的图像）等问题。虽然验证提示能改善错误检测，但改善幅度有限，且缺乏对作者偏见的敏感性。该研究提示需要谨慎对待LLM在同行评审自动化中的应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate peer reviews, prompting examination of their capacity for critical evaluation. This study evaluates two multimodal LLMs, Qwen2.5-VL-72B and Pixtral-Large-124B, as reviewers across 165 submissions to the 2026 International Conference on Learning Representations, a venue that postdates both models' training cutoffs. Manuscripts were presented to both models with author identities blinded, replaced with high-prestige affiliations, or replaced with low-prestige affiliations, and in either text-only or text-with-figure format. Additionally, 145 verifiably detectable errors were inserted into 55 manuscripts to assess error identification under natural and verification-oriented prompts. Across all manuscript groups, including rejected submissions, LLM scores ranged from 7.0 to 8.1, whereas human mean scores ranged from 3.4 to 6.8. The models detected 12.1\% of the verified errors under natural prompting, and a one-sentence verification instruction increased detection to 22.2\%; however, 78\% of the errors remained undetected. Providing figures reduced error detection while increasing review scores. No visual error was reliably verified against its corresponding figure, and half of the text-only reviews described figures that were not provided. Author identity did not influence either review scores or error detection. LLM editorial decisions exactly matched those produced by simple score averaging.

</details>


### [88] [Intelligent Identification and Repair of Design Defects in BIM via Domain-Specific Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28629)
*Jia-Rui Lin,Yun-Hong Cai,Xiang-Rui Ni,Peng Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于领域特定大语言模型（LLM）的集成框架，用于识别和修复BIM中的各类设计缺陷。通过BIM-to-Text转换、提示学习（含规则注入、少样本提示和RAG）以及幻觉控制策略，实现了85%的缺陷识别准确率（对比传统规则检查的70%）和94%的合理修复建议率，幻觉控制将准确率从64%提升至85%并消除92.5%的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏通用化的途径来高效识别和解决BIM中多样化的设计缺陷，需要一种能够灵活应对不同类型缺陷的集成化方法。

Method: 该方法包括三个核心部分：(1) 提出组件均衡分块的BIM-to-Text方法，将BIM数据与LLM桥接；(2) 采用规则注入、少样本提示和RAG相结合的提示学习策略来识别缺陷并生成修复建议；(3) 引入结合关键标识验证和token长度阈值的幻觉控制策略以确保输出可靠性。最终构建了从原始BIM数据输入到缺陷识别再到修复建议生成的端到端原型。

Result: 实验结果表明：缺陷识别准确率达85%，优于传统规则检查的70%；修复建议合理率达到94%；幻觉控制策略将准确率从64%提升至85%，单轮干预即消除92.5%的幻觉。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了从原始BIM数据输入到修复建议生成的端到端原型，验证了基于领域LLM的集成框架在BIM缺陷识别与修复方面的有效性和可靠性。

Abstract: Existing methods lack a generalized approach to efficiently identify and resolve the diversity of design defects in BIM. Therefore, this study proposes an integrated framework to identify and repair various defects in BIM via domain-specific LLMs. Firstly, a BIM-to-Text method with component-balanced chunking is introduced to bridge BIM data with LLMs. Then, prompt learning with rule injection, few-shot prompting and RAG is proposed to identify defects and generate repair suggestions. Meanwhile, a hallucination control strategy combining key identifier validation and token-length thresholds is introduced to ensure reliability. Experiments show capability expansion yields 85% identification accuracy versus 70% for traditional rule checking, achieving a 94% rate of reasonable repair suggestions. Moreover, the proposed hallucination control further increased accuracy from 64% to 85%, eliminating 92.5% of hallucinations in a single intervention round. This study establishes an end-to-end prototype from raw BIM data input, through defect identification, to repair suggestion generation.

</details>


### [89] [Enabling Proactive Spoken Turns via a Generalized Style-Aware Full-Duplex Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28630)
*Tianrui Pan,Qinglin Zhang,Chong Deng,Luyao Cheng,Qian Chen,Wen Wang,Jie Tang,Gangshan Wu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用的、风格可控的全双工对话框架，包含三个核心组件：轻量级主动语音轮次控制器LPS-TC（支持即插即用集成）、大规模真实世界多轮立体声对话数据集WildTurn（约2981小时，含五类轮次转换和五类回声行为风格标注），以及一个双层评估体系（块级时序精度和轮次级交互质量）。实验表明该框架在时序恰当性和响应质量上均优于现有系统，且具备细粒度风格可控性和强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然全双工对话系统要求智能体能够实时主动行动（如打断、回声反馈），而现有半双工系统仅等待用户说完再响应。核心挑战在于在不牺牲响应质量的前提下改善轮次时序（turn timing），现有系统在真实场景的主动轮次转换能力上存在明显不足。

Method: 提出三组件框架：(1) LPS-TC——轻量级主动语音轮次控制器，具有细粒度的动作空间（同时覆盖反应性和主动性轮次行为），可即插即用增强半双工和全双工模型；(2) WildTurn——约2981小时的筛选后真实世界多轮立体声英语对话数据集（含面对面和电话对话），标注了五种轮次转换风格和五种回声行为风格；(3) 双层评估方案——分别在块级（chunk-level）时序精度和轮次级（turn-level）交互质量上进行评估，模拟真实流式场景约束。

Result: 将LPS-TC与半双工模型Qwen2.5-Omni及全双工模型Freeze-Omni集成，实验证明其在时序恰当性和响应质量上均表现优越，同时展现了细粒度的风格可控性和强大的泛化能力。相比现有静态全双工基准，LPS-TC展现出更丰富的口语动态特性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过即插即用的轻量级控制器和大规模风格标注数据集，有效地弥合了半双工与全双工对话系统之间的差距，使语音交互更加自然和类似人类，为改善全双工对话的主动轮次时序提供了可行且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Compared with half-duplex dialogue systems where the system waits for user turn completion before it responds, natural full-duplex dialogue systems require agents to act proactively in real time, including timely interruptions and backchannels. This creates a key challenge: improving turn timing without sacrificing response quality. To address limitations in realistic proactive turn-taking, we build a generalized style-aware full-duplex framework with three key components. Firstly, we propose LPS-TC, a Lightweight Proactive Speech Turn Controller for plug-and-play integration. It features a fine-grained action space covering both reactive and proactive turn behaviors, enabling half-duplex models with full-duplex capabilities and enhancing existing full-duplex models with superior timing control. Secondly, we construct WildTurn, a large-scale, real-world English dataset containing approximately 2,981 hours of filtered multi-turn stereo conversations from face-to-face and telephone conversations, annotated with five turn-taking and five backchanneling styles. Trained on WildTurn, LPS-TC exhibits rich spoken dynamics that are not captured by existing static full-duplex benchmarks. Thirdly, we introduce a two-tier evaluation scheme that assesses both chunk-level timing precision and turn-level interaction quality under realistic streaming constraints. Our experiments, integrating LPS-TC with half-duplex models like Qwen2.5-Omni and full-duplex models like Freeze-Omni, showcase its superior performance in timing appropriateness and response quality. Our framework also demonstrates fine-grained style controllability and strong generalizability, enabling more natural and human-like spoken interactions.

</details>


### [90] [PAUSE: Editable Strategy Artifacts for Long-Form Cultural Story Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28633)
*Taaha Kazi,Vasu Sharma,Mohammad Saifullah,Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAUSE（暂停-更新策略编辑）干预方法，将AI媒介下的文化改编策略暴露为可编辑的人工控制界面。在两部中文源长篇连载小说中，9个对比章节里人类对策略的编辑全部传播到章节级散文，目标标记出现在8/9个编辑输出中且0/9个对照组出现，禁止标记则相反。作者将此定位为小规模编辑遵循性研究，而非文化权威性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI日益介入文化改编过程，但其文化决策往往隐藏在提示词、临时模型计划或最终文本中，导致人类难以检查、干预和争议这些决策。尤其在长篇小说改编场景下，文化决策一旦生成便贯穿后续生成流程，缺乏可操作性的人机控制界面。

Method: 提出PAUSE（Pause-And-Update Strategy Editing）干预方法：将编辑策略构建为结构化工件，使其可被检查、编辑，并通过下游角色、实体和章节定位阶段投射到散文输出中。在两部中文源长篇连载小说上进行编辑遵循性实验，通过9组编辑版与对照版章节对比，采用裁判评估和标记审计两种方式验证编辑传播效果。

Result: 在9组编辑版与对照版章节对比中，裁判全部（9/9）选择编辑策略输出；标记审计显示目标标记出现在8/9个编辑输出中而0/9个对照组出现，禁止标记从编辑输出中消失且存在于全部对照组中。

Conclusion: 研究为小规模（smoke-scale）编辑遵循性验证，表明PAUSE提供了一种实用途径，使AI媒介下的文化改编在决策传播到长文本生成之前，能够更可检查和可争议。但作者明确不主张输出具有文化权威性或文学质量提升。

Abstract: Generative AI systems increasingly mediate cultural adaptation, but their cultural decisions are often hidden inside prompts, transient model plans, or final prose. We study PAUSE (Pause-And-Update Strategy Editing), an intervention that exposes an editable adaptation strategy as a human control surface for cultural decisions in long-form story adaptation. The strategy is a structured artifact that can be inspected, edited, and then projected through downstream character, entity, and chapter-localization stages. In two Chinese-source serialized novels, we test whether human edits to this strategy propagate into chapter-level prose. Across 9 edited-vs-control chapter comparisons, judges select the edited-strategy output in all 9; a marker audit shows target markers in 8/9 edited outputs and 0/9 controls, with forbidden markers absent from edited outputs and present in all controls. We frame these results as a smoke-scale edit-adherence study, not a claim that the outputs are culturally authoritative or literary-quality improvements. PAUSE offers one practical way to make AI-mediated cultural adaptation more inspectable and contestable before decisions propagate through long-form generation.

</details>


### [91] [Do MLLMs Really Understand Low-Resource Khmer Documents? A Pilot Study on Khmer Document VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28635)
*Nimol Thuon,Panhapin Theang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对开放多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在高棉语（Khmer）文档图像上的能力进行了试点诊断评估。构建了基于KH-FUNSD数据集的评估子集，覆盖发票、收据、报价单等商业表单，包含英/柬双语问题及多种答案形式。在Qwen-VL系列模型上测试了直接图像提示、解析器辅助和外部OCR辅助三种配置，结果显示直接Qwen3-VL-8B达到51.9%准确率，而外部OCR辅助（Tesseract 61.9%，PaddleOCR 61.6%）表现最佳；但高棉语脚本答案仍显著难于英文和数字字段，表明当前MLLMs对本地高棉语文档的可靠理解仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在文档理解、视觉问答和文本提取方面取得了显著进展，但其在低资源、非拉丁语系场景下的可靠性仍不明确。高棉语表单文档因包含复杂文字形态、混合高棉语-英语字段以及柬埔寨瑞尔和美元两种货币值而尤为具有挑战性，且高棉语文档VQA的可用资源非常有限，亟需评估现有模型的真实能力与失败模式。

Method: 从已有的KH-FUNSD数据集中构建评估子集，涵盖发票、收据、报价单和其他商业表单。评估子集包含英语和高棉语两种语言的问题，答案保留原始形式（英语、高棉语、混合脚本或数字）。采用三种配置进行评估：多种开放的Qwen-VL模型的直接基于图像的提示，以及Qwen3-VL-8B的解析器辅助配置和外部OCR辅助配置。

Result: 直接的Qwen3-VL-8B优于更小模型，总体准确率达到51.9%，但高棉语脚本和混合脚本答案的性能仍然有限。外部OCR辅助配置产生最强结果，Tesseract达到61.9%，PaddleOCR达到61.6%。然而，高棉语脚本答案仍然比英语和数字字段困难得多。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs能够处理视觉清晰的英语和结构化数字内容，但对本地高棉语文档的可靠理解仍然是一个尚未解决的开放挑战。该研究作为试点诊断评估，而非引入完整的公共基准，旨在揭示现有模型的失败模式并为未来改进提供方向。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced document understanding, visual question answering, and text extraction. However, their reliability in low-resource, non-Latin settings remains uncertain. Khmer form documents present particular challenges because they contain complex script forms, mixed Khmer-English fields, and monetary values in both Cambodian Riel and US Dollars. Available resources for Khmer Document VQA are also limited. This paper presents a pilot diagnostic evaluation of open MLLMs on Khmer document images. We construct an evaluation subset from the previously introduced KH-FUNSD collection, covering invoices, receipts, quotations, and other business forms. The subset includes questions in English and Khmer, with answers retained in their original English, Khmer, mixed-script, or numeric forms. Rather than introducing a full public benchmark, this study examines the capabilities and failure modes of existing models. We evaluate representative open Qwen-VL models using direct image-based prompting and compare parser-assisted and external OCR-assisted configurations with Qwen3-VL-8B. Direct Qwen3-VL-8B outperforms smaller models, achieving 51.9% overall accuracy, although performance remains limited for Khmer-script and mixed-script answers. External OCR produces the strongest results, reaching 61.9% with Tesseract and 61.6% with PaddleOCR. Nevertheless, Khmer-script answers remain substantially more difficult than English and numeric fields. The results indicate that current MLLMs can process visually clear English and structured numeric content, but reliable native Khmer document understanding remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [92] [PromptKWS: A Novel Prompt-Guided Open-Vocabulary Keyword Spotting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28640)
*Gaopeng Xu,Chengfei Li,Xianliang Wang,Lin Zhu,Juan Wei,Wenpeng Li,Jianwei Niu,Jie Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PromptKWS框架，通过提示短语预测网络(PPN)提取关键词提示嵌入，并利用提示-声学多头交叉注意力将提示嵌入融合到KWS编码器中，实现开放式词汇关键词唤醒，在唤醒率和复杂环境（噪声、发音变异）下准确率上均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放式词汇关键词唤醒(KWS)系统在准确率上仍有不足，尤其在复杂的真实环境（噪声、发音变异）下，纯声学模型表现不佳，亟需一种能够有效利用关键词提示信息来提升KWS系统鲁棒性和准确率的方案。

Method: 构建PromptKWS框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 提示短语预测网络(PPN)，采用编码器-解码器架构，用于提取关键词提示的嵌入表示；(2) 提示-声学多头交叉注意力(MHCA)机制，将PPN编码器得到的提示嵌入注入到提示引导的KWS编码器中，实现关键词提示与声学特征的融合。

Result: 实验表明，PromptKWS相比基线系统将唤醒率提升了超过10%；在涉及噪声和发音变异的复杂真实世界场景中，相比纯声学模型，PromptKWS在测试集上平均准确率提升超过15%。

Conclusion: PromptKWS通过提示引导的方式有效提升了开放式词汇KWS系统的准确率和鲁棒性，尤其在复杂声学环境下优势显著，验证了利用关键词提示嵌入增强声学模型的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present PromptKWS, a novel Prompt-guided keyword spotting (KWS) framework to improve the accuracy of open vocabulary KWS systems. In specific terms, we introduce the Prompt Phrases Prediction Network (PPN), an encoder-decoder architecture designed to effectively extract keyword prompts embeddings. we employ the PPN encoder to encode the keyword prompts and infuse the prompt embedding into the Prompt-guided KWS encoder by utilizing a Prompt-acoustic Multi-head Cross-attention (MHCA). Experiments show that PromptKWS improves the wakeup rate by over 10% compared to baseline system. Notably, another strength of PromptKWS is its ability to effectively leverage keyword prompts for adapting to complex real-world environments involving noise and pronunciation variations. In comparison to purely acoustic models, which often struggle in such situations, PromptKWS demonstrates remarkable performance, with an average accuracy improvement of over 15% in test sets.

</details>


### [93] [Terminal-Bench-LILT: Multilingual Agentic Coding Benchmark Grounded in Language, Region, and Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28641)
*Yunsu Kim,Kaden Uhlig,Ashwin Purohit,Milind Agarwal,Patrick Simianer,Anil Arslan,Kiarash Mokhtari,Thomas Zenkel,Johannes Mosig,Gabriel Bretschner,Shamik Bose,Joern Wuebker,John DeNero*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Terminal-Bench-LILT是一个包含10种非英语语言、300个真实编码任务的评测基准，用于评估编码智能体在多语言软件开发环境中的能力。评测发现即使最强模型通过率仅63.1%，且多语言编码能力是一个独立于通用基准排名的重要能力维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的编码智能体评测几乎全部基于英语，无法反映真实世界的多语言部署场景。作者认为多语言编码能力是一个被忽视的能力轴，需要专门的评测基准来衡量。

Method: 构建包含10种语言（阿拉伯语、捷克语、德语、西班牙语、印地语、日语、韩语、塞尔维亚语、土耳其语、中文）的300个真实编码任务，每个任务针对非英语软件开发特有的问题（如国际化、编码、文本规范化、文化惯例），任务由母语程序员编写并通过多阶段质量控制流水线验证。

Result: 评估了6个前沿模型，最强模型通过率仅63.1%，许多任务没有任何模型能解决。性能在不同语言间差异显著，且与通用编码基准排名不相关。

Conclusion: 多语言编码能力是一个独特且尚未充分探索的能力维度，构建更全面的多语言编码评测对于推动编码智能体在实际部署场景中的表现具有重要意义。

Abstract: Most evaluations for coding agents are conducted exclusively in English, which does not reflect real-world multilingual deployment. We present Terminal-Bench-LILT, a suite of 300 authentic coding tasks in ten languages: Arabic, Czech, German, Spanish, Hindi, Japanese, Korean, Serbian, Turkish, and Chinese. Each task targets issues specific to non-English software development that have no direct English equivalent, e.g., internationalization, encoding, text normalization, and cultural conventions. All tasks are authored by native-speaker programmers and validated through a multi-stage quality control pipeline. Evaluation of six frontier models reveals that even the strongest model reaches only 63.1\% pass rate, with many tasks unsolved by any model. Performance varies substantially by language and does not track general coding benchmark rankings, highlighting that multilingual coding competence is a distinct and underexplored capability axis. Sample tasks are available at https://github.com/lilt/terminal-bench-lilt

</details>


### [94] [Redesigning and Auditing Deep Research Writing for Faithful Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28643)
*Hiroaki Hayashi,Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Prafulla Kumar Choubey,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CLAIMPROBE，一种用于深度研究（DR）系统报告细粒度审计的声明级评估框架，可分解报告为声明并测量幻觉、错误归因、引用卫生和必要事实召回。作者发现强DR流水线即使在rubric分数稳定的情况下仍会遗漏关键证据和错误归因。进而提出CLAIMWRITER，一种分层声明级写作框架，从源事实提取、映射到查询推导的提纲，并从源链接声明表示起草各章节。将CLAIMWRITER替换为三个DR框架的写作模块后，幻觉减少2.6至4.5倍，必要事实召回提升1.2至1.7倍，同时保持整体报告质量；CLAIMWRITER还支持局部修订，在源变化时以最高速率传播变更事实，且更具成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于rubric的深度研究报告评估无法揭示报告中的细粒度事实性失败，导致即使评分稳定，报告仍可能存在关键证据遗漏和错误归因等问题，需要一个更精细的声明级审计框架来评估和改善DR系统的可靠性。

Method: 1) 提出CLAIMPROBE审计框架，将DR报告分解为声明并对照检索证据测量幻觉、错误归因、引用卫生和必要事实召回四个维度。2) 提出CLAIMWRITER分层声明级写作框架，从源文档提取源事实，映射至查询推导的提纲，构建源链接的声明表示，并据此分节起草报告。3) CLAIMWRITER支持局部修订机制，源变化时仅传播变更的源事实进行针对性重写。

Result: 在三个前DR框架中将报告写作模块替换为CLAIMWRITER后，幻觉率降低2.6至4.5倍，必要事实召回提升1.2至1.7倍，同时整体报告质量基本保持不变。在源变化场景下，CLAIMWRITER的变更事实传播率最高，且成本效益优于现有更新方法。

Conclusion: 声明级的审计（CLAIMPROBE）和写作（CLAIMWRITER）设计能够显著改善深度研究系统的事实准确性，克服rubric评估的盲区，并在报告更新场景中实现高效、成本可控的局部修订。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluations of deep-research (DR) systems often obscure fine-grained factual failures in generated reports. We introduce CLAIMPROBE, a claim-level audit that decomposes DR reports into claims and measures hallucination, misattribution, citation hygiene, and necessary-fact recall against retrieved evidence. Using CLAIMPROBE, we find that strong DR pipelines can omit key evidence and misattribute claims even when their rubric scores remain stable. We then propose CLAIMWRITER, a hierarchical claim-based writer that extracts source facts, maps them to a query-derived outline, and drafts each section from a source-linked claim representation. Across three prior DR frameworks, replacing only the report writer with CLAIMWRITER reduces hallucination by 2.6 to 4.5 times and improves necessary-fact recall by 1.2 to 1.7 times, while largely preserving overall report quality. CLAIMWRITER also enables localized revision: when sources change, it propagates changed source facts into revised reports at the highest rate among update methods, while also being more cost-effective.

</details>


### [95] [Cross Lingual Transfer in Tulu Legal Comprehension: Script-Dependent Improvement and RAG-Induced Knowledge Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28645)
*Sindhu Shetty,Spurthi Setty,Natan Vidra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在低资源跨语言环境下，利用高资源相关语言（如卡纳达语）作为脚手架来帮助模型理解低资源达罗毗荼语言（图卢语）的法律投诉文本。三个模型（Llama3、Hex-1、Sarvam）在零训练条件下测试分类能力，发现音译查询的效果高度依赖脚本，RAG框架下检索卡纳达语法律语料产生混合结果，失败模式主要源于事实替换和虚构（幻觉）。研究定位推理失败源于模型对信息的解析和推理过程，而非语料内容本身，并提出统计诚实性框架和推理轨迹分析作为更广泛适用的低资源多语言RAG评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言缺乏充足的训练语料，通常借助相关的高资源语言作为理解和分析的支架。然而，在跨语言低资源环境中缺乏严格的评估方法来识别模型的失败模式，特别是在法律领域这一高风险场景下，需要进行系统性研究来标注模型能力边界。

Method: 以法律投诉分类任务为测试场景，使用三种模型（Llama3、Hex-1、Sarvam）对低资源达罗毗荼语言图卢语（Tulu）的投诉文本进行分类。采用跨达罗毗荼脚本的音译策略实现零训练理解，并在RAG框架下从卡纳达语法律文书语料库中检索增强上下文。结合推理轨迹分析和统计诚实性框架对失败原因进行诊断。

Result: 音译后模型能初步理解投诉内容，但理解程度高度依赖脚本选择（卡纳达语效果最强）。RAG检索结果混合：部分模型在特定条件下有弱正向趋势，失败时集中于两类错误——事实替换（固着于特定摘录段落导致推理偏斜）和虚构（与查询和语料均无依据的幻觉）。模型对信息的解析和推理是失败根源，而非语料内容。

Conclusion: 在低资源领域，模型的理解失败源于信息解析和推理环节而非语料内容；脚本依赖的理解能力与RAG鲁棒性呈正相关关系。所提出的推理轨迹分析和统计诚实性框架作为低资源多语言RAG评估工具具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Low-resource languages without an adequate training corpus often use a related, higher-resource language as a scaffold for comprehension. Still, there is a need to develop rigorous evaluation methods to identify when models fail in cross lingual low-resource environments. Using the legal domain as a backdrop, three models (Llama3, Hex-1, Sarvam) were tested on the ability to classify legal complaints written in a low resource Dravidian language (Tulu). Transliterating queries across Dravidian scripts allowed models to gain a preliminary understanding of speakers' complaints without the use of wide scale training, though the level of comprehension was heavily script dependent (with Kannada - another relatively low-resource language - producing the strongest positive trend). Retrieving from a corpus of Kannada legal papers across a RAG framework caused mixed results. Some models had a weak positive trend in comprehension under certain conditions, but when models failed, it was often across two axes: fact substitution (fixating on specific passage excerpts that skewed reasoning) and confabulation (hallucination that had no basis in either query or corpus). Within low resource domains, results identify the model's parsing of information and subsequent reasoning as the source of reasoning failure, rather than corpus contents. Script-dependent comprehension and RAG robustness also seem to travel together. This is further supported by the reasoning-trace analysis and a statistical-honesty framework deployed - techniques that are more broadly applicable to low-resource multilingual RAG evaluation.

</details>


### [96] [Can Large Language Models Identify Meaningful Touchpoints in Conversion Attribution?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28649)
*Jinqi Wu,Sishuo Chen,Zhangming Chan,Yong Bai,Chao Yi,Han Zhu,Shuodian Yu,Lei Zhang,Sheng Chen,Chenghuan Hou,Jian Xu,Chaoyou Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究转化归因中的触点选择问题，指出现有基于协同过滤的方法忽略了用户感知的语义意图，通过人类标注揭示语义鸿沟后，系统评估了LLM在识别隐含相关触点方面的能力，并利用LLM标注的转化标签提升工业CVR模型训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转化归因中的触点选择方法严重依赖基于协同过滤的启发式规则，无法与用户感知的语义意图对齐。通过人工标注发现，大量隐式相关的语义触点被现有规则遗漏，存在显著的语义鸿沟，亟需探索新的选择范式。

Method: 首先通过人类标注揭示现有方法与用户语义意图之间的差距；然后系统评估LLM在识别隐式相关触点方面的能力，分析不同提示策略和基础模型选择对识别性能的影响；最后将LLM归因的转化标签用于增强工业CVR模型训练。

Result: LLM能够有效发现大部分隐式相关触点，但选择性能仍有显著提升空间；不同提示策略和基础模型在识别表现上存在差异；利用LLM归因标签训练CVR模型获得了显著的离线性能提升。

Conclusion: LLM在转化归因中展示了从机械规则匹配向人类对齐的语义推理转变的潜力，为转化归因领域提供了新的技术路线图。

Abstract: Touchpoint selection in conversion attribution, namely identifying meaningful touchpoints contributing to conversions, is essential for e-commerce recommendation and online advertising. Current selection methods rely heavily on collaborative-filtering-based heuristics, which fail to align with user-perceived semantic intent. Through human annotation, we reveal a significant semantic gap: many implicitly-related, semantically relevant touchpoints remain undetected by existing rules. Therefore, we systematically evaluate the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) in identifying these hidden associations. Our evaluation shows that while LLMs effectively uncover a substantial portion of implicitly-related touchpoints, significant room for improvement remains in their selection performance. Furthermore, we analyze the impact of different prompting strategies and foundation model choices on identification performance, providing valuable insights into their reasoning patterns and effectiveness. These insights offer a new roadmap for transitioning conversion attribution from mechanical rule-matching to human-aligned semantic reasoning. Moreover, we leverage the LLM-attributed conversion labels for enhancing industrial CVR model training and achieve significant offline performance gains, showing the potential of LLMs in conversion attribution.

</details>


### [97] [Test-Time Scaling for Scientific Equation Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28660)
*Haowei Lin,Hubert Lim,Xiangyu Wang,Letian Huang,Di He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了测试时计算扩展（TTS）在自动方程发现这一开放式任务中的应用。作者将LLM驱动的方程发现统一建模为迭代搜索过程，涵盖Best-of-N、序列细化、树搜索和进化方法，并在固定预算下比较最小并行控制器以隔离计算分配效应。实验发现搜索宽度是主导分配参数，其最优值随计算预算增加，合理选择宽度还能提升墙钟效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算扩展研究主要集中在封闭式任务（如数学和编码），而对开放式任务（如方程发现）的TTS研究不足。作者希望理解在开放式设置中计算分配策略对模型搜索性能的影响，并隔离分配效应与其他启发式因素。

Method: 将LLM驱动的方程发现表述为一个迭代搜索过程，在统一的计算分配视角下涵盖Best-of-N、序列细化、树搜索和进化方法。采用最小并行控制器在固定计算预算下进行比较，在LLM-SRBench方程发现任务上系统扫描搜索宽度、种群-分支划分比例和控制器类型等参数。

Result: 搜索宽度是最主要的分配参数，其最优值通常随计算预算增加而增大；种群-分支划分比例和控制器选择影响较小。适当选择宽度还能通过增加并行度提升墙钟效率。

Conclusion: 在拥有信息充分验证器的情况下，控制探索与利用之间的平衡是扩展基于LLM的方程发现的关键；搜索宽度作为主导参数应优先关注。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves language model reasoning by allocating additional test-time compute, but prior work mainly studies closed-ended tasks such as math and coding. We study TTS for automated equation discovery, an open-ended setting where models search over candidate equations and rely on observed datapoints for feedback. We formulate LLM-driven equation discovery as an iterative search process that unifies Best-of-N, sequential refinement, tree search, and evolution-style methods under a common compute-allocation view. To isolate allocation effects from prompt engineering and other heuristics, we compare minimal parallel controllers under fixed budgets. On LLM-SRBench equation-discovery tasks, we find that search width is the dominant allocation parameter: the best width in our sweep generally increases with the compute budget, while the population--branching split and controller choice matter less. Appropriate width selection also improves wall-clock efficiency by increasing parallelism. These results suggest that, given an informative verifier, controlling exploration and exploitation is central to scaling LLM-based equation discovery.

</details>


### [98] [GreenBench: Benchmarking Energy Efficiency and Carbon Footprint of Open-Source LLM Inference on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28667)
*Rajeswari Kannan,Raj Firke,Shreya Bengle,Srushti Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GreenBench基准测试框架，系统评估了Apple M4 Pro（48GB统一内存）上五个开源LLM（3-9B参数）在三个NLP任务中的能源效率、吞吐量和碳足迹。研究发现M4 Pro在持续推理时CPU+GPU封装功耗仅0.47W，系统总功耗8-12W，单用户部署时每token能效比数据中心GPU高30-40倍；较小模型（3-3.8B）比较大模型（7-9B）吞吐量高2.6-4.2倍，每token能耗最高节省62%。Pareto分析显示Qwen 2.5（7B）在57% MMLU和59 tokens/s下实现最佳精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的环境影响日益受到关注，但现有Green AI研究主要聚焦于数据中心GPU和嵌入式平台，对Apple Silicon（统一内存架构）的LLM推理能耗特征尚未有人研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供Apple Silicon平台上的系统性能耗基准数据。

Method: 作者开发了GreenBench基准测试框架，在Apple M4 Pro（48GB统一内存）上评估五个开源LLM（3-9B参数）在三个NLP任务上的表现。使用macOS powermetrics进行直接功耗测量，利用Ollama的纳秒级精度计时工具，从封装级和系统级两个层面记录每token能耗，并结合印度和美国电网数据估算CO2排放。

Result: M4 Pro在持续推理时CPU+GPU封装功耗仅0.47W，系统总功耗8-12W，单用户部署下每token能效比数据中心GPU高30-40倍。较小模型（3-3.8B）吞吐量比大模型（7-9B）高2.6-4.2倍，每token能耗最高降低62%。Pareto分析表明Qwen 2.5（7B）提供最佳精度-效率权衡（57% MMLU，59 tokens/s），Llama 3.2（3B）适合延迟敏感场景（175 tokens/s）。

Conclusion: Apple Silicon凭借其统一内存架构在单用户LLM推理场景中展现出极高的能效优势，小型化模型在边缘部署中具有显著的能效潜力。GreenBench框架为Apple Silicon平台上的绿色AI研究提供了标准化基准工具，本文成果对模型选择和部署策略优化具有实用参考价值。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Large Language Models (LLMs) has raised concerns about their environmental impact during inference. While Green AI research has focused on datacenter GPUs and embedded platforms, the energy profile of LLM inference on Apple Silicon, with its unified memory architecture, remains unstudied. This paper presents GreenBench, a benchmarking framework that evaluates the energy efficiency, throughput, and carbon footprint of five open-source LLMs (3-9B parameters) across three NLP tasks on an Apple M4 Pro with 48 GB unified memory. Using macOS powermetrics for direct power measurement and Ollama's nanosecond-precision timing, we find that the M4 Pro draws only 0.47 W of CPU+GPU package power during sustained inference, with total system power of 8-12 W, achieving 30-40x better energy efficiency per token than datacenter GPUs in single-user deployment. Smaller models (3-3.8B) deliver 2.6-4.2x higher throughput and up to 62% less energy per token than larger models (7-9B). Pareto analysis identifies Qwen 2.5 (7B) as the optimal accuracy-efficiency trade-off at 57% MMLU and 59 tokens/s, while Llama 3.2 (3B) suits latency-critical applications at 175 tokens/s. We provide per-token energy at package and system levels with CO2 estimates for India and US grids.

</details>


### [99] [ReVA: A Region-Aware Visual Assistant for Visually Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28707)
*Anoop Senthil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReVA是一个区域感知的视觉问答模型，通过双桥（图像桥+区域桥）将CLIP ViT-L/14和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct连接起来，融合整图级与区域级表示，显式注入细粒度区域证据以减少MLLM的物体幻觉并提升事实性接地能力。在POPE上达到82.85%的平均F1，优于无区域token的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在VQA中缺乏精确的空间推理与细粒度视觉理解能力，常因区域级与细粒度视觉接地不足而产生物体、属性和空间幻觉，即模型以高置信度生成缺乏视觉依据的回答。

Method: ReVA采用冻结的CLIP ViT-L/14视觉编码器与Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct LLM，通过双桥进行对齐：(1) 图像桥将最终transformer块特征映射为图像token；(2) 区域桥将ViT各层富集的中间特征裁剪映射为每个边界框的K个区域token，使早期纹理和后期物体线索更显著。检测器栈（RAM++、spaCy、Grounding DINO）提供零样本、问题无关与问题相关的边界框。图像token与区域token拼接作为LLM提示前缀，联合编码场景级上下文与细粒度区域证据。

Result: 在VQAv2、MMBench、POPE和SEED-Bench上进行评估，ReVA在POPE上达到82.85%的平均F1，而仅有图像token的基线为81.14%，表明区域感知的视觉表示能有效减少物体幻觉。

Conclusion: 显式引入区域感知的视觉表示能够减少物体幻觉并改善MLLM的事实性接地，验证了区域级视觉证据在提升多模态LLM细粒度空间理解中的关键作用。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in Visual Question Answering (VQA), yet they continue to struggle with questions requiring precise spatial reasoning and fine-grained visual understanding. These limitations often manifest as object, attribute, and spatial hallucinations, where models generate confident but visually unsupported responses due to insufficient region-level and fine-grained visual grounding. To address this challenge, we propose ReVA, a region-aware VQA model that employs a frozen CLIP ViT-L/14 Vision Transformer (ViT) and a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct large language model (LLM) connected through a dual bridge that aligns both whole-image and region-level representations with the LLM's embedding space. The image bridge maps final transformer block features into image tokens. The region bridge maps cropped features from enriched intermediate features across ViT blocks so early texture and later object cues are more evident, into K region tokens for every bounding box. ReVA uses a detector stack that supplies automatic zero-shot bounding boxes that are both question-agnostic and question-dependent, using RAM++ (Recognize Anything Model), spaCy, and Grounding DINO. The image tokens and region tokens are concatenated as an LLM prompt prefix to jointly encode scene-level context and fine-grained regional evidence when answering questions. Evaluated on VQAv2, MMBench, POPE, and SEED-Bench, ReVA achieves 82.85% mean F1 on POPE, compared with 81.14% for an image-token baseline without region tokens. These results demonstrate that explicit region-aware visual representations reduce object hallucination and improve the factual grounding of MLLMs.

</details>


### [100] [Evaluating Multilingual Sentence Embeddings for Translation Error Detection:An English--Greek Contrastive Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28776)
*Eleftherios Kalogeros,Athanasios Ntalakas,Manolis Gergatsoulis,Paschalis Nikolaou,Sotiria-Lito Alexaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个覆盖十五类错误类别的英-希对比数据集（含1,850个示例），评估五种多语言句子嵌入模型（BGE-M3、Multilingual E5、Multilingual MPNet、LaBSE、Jina Embeddings v3）及COMETKiwi基线对细微翻译错误的敏感度。BGE-M3以89.30%的对比准确率领先嵌入模型，COMETKiwi达94.49%。嵌入模型在显式事实与词汇变化上表现好，但难以检测时体与代词共指错误；两类方法呈现互补的错误敏感性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言句子嵌入被广泛用于跨语言语义相似度估计，但其对细粒度翻译错误的敏感性尚未被充分理解，填补这一研究空白有助于更可靠地使用这些模型进行翻译评估。

Method: 基于FLORES+句对齐参考译文开发对比数据集，由两名翻译专家审校，涵盖十个核心与五个探索性错误类别共1,850个示例；使用余弦相似度评估五个多语言句子嵌入模型及COMETKiwi参考无关质量评估基线，通过对比准确率和分数边际衡量各类别敏感性。

Result: BGE-M3在嵌入模型中准确率最高（89.30%），COMETKiwi达94.49%。嵌入模型对显式事实和词汇变化检测可靠，但对时体及代词共指错误敏感度低；COMETKiwi在时体、代词共指和语义角色等困难类别上表现更优，但在日期时间错误和数字处理上不及嵌入模型。

Conclusion: 多语言句子嵌入与COMETKiwi具有互补的错误敏感性特征：嵌入模型可提供有用的语义充分性信号，但更适合作为更广泛翻译评估框架的组成部分，而非独立评估指标。

Abstract: Multilingual sentence embeddings are increasingly used to estimate semantic similarity across languages, yet their sensitivity to fine-grained translation errors remains insufficiently understood. This study investigates whether general-purpose multilingual embedding models can distinguish correct English-Greek translations from minimally modified erroneous alternatives. A contrastive dataset was developed from FLORES+ sentence-aligned reference translations and reviewed by two translation experts. It contains 1,850 examples across ten core and five exploratory error categories, covering factual, lexical-semantic, grammatical, relational, referential, and discourse-level phenomena.
  Five multilingual sentence-embedding models (BGE-M3, Multilingual E5, Multilingual MPNet, LaBSE, and Jina Embeddings v3) were evaluated using cosine similarity between each English source sentence and its correct and erroneous Greek translations. A reference-free COMETKiwi model was also evaluated as an MT quality-estimation baseline. Performance was assessed through contrastive accuracy and score margins for category-specific sensitivity. BGE-M3 achieved the highest accuracy among embedding models at 89.30 percent, while COMETKiwi achieved 94.49 percent. Embedding models detected explicit factual and lexical changes more reliably than tense-and-aspect and pronoun-coreference errors. COMETKiwi improved performance on several difficult categories, including tense and aspect, pronoun and coreference, and semantic-role errors, but showed lower sensitivity to date-and-time errors and underperformed the embedding models on numbers. The results show complementary error-sensitivity profiles: multilingual sentence embeddings provide useful semantic adequacy signals but are better suited as components of broader translation-evaluation frameworks than as standalone metrics.

</details>


### [101] [A rigor-matched audit of periodic-step layer skipping for efficient llm inference: conflayers versus swift, with a supplemental analysis of trained routing alternatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28846)
*Prateek Kumar Sikdar,Arpan Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对两种周期性步进、在线搜索的层跳转推理方法（置信门控早退基线 ConfLayers 与真实自投机解码 SWIFT）以及传统自回归解码，在两个模型规模（Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B）和两个任务（GSM8K、CNN/DailyMail）上进行了严格的三种子审计。结果显示 SWIFT 在四种组合中三种准确率最强，且分离在线搜索开销后推理速度在全部四格中比 ConfLayers 快 5-21%；ConfLayers 全面落败，尤其在 1.5B GSM8K 上差距显著。补充分析还评估了 LayerRoute 和 LayerDrop 两种粗粒度训练路由方法，两者虽有 1.08-1.33 倍加速但准确率远低于周期性方法（LayerRoute 在 1.5B GSM8K 上近乎崩溃，EM 均值仅 0.003）。作者发布了完整审计协议作为效率对比模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层跳转方法在高效 LLM 推理中越来越受关注，但现有评测缺乏严格的、方法间可比性与可信度匹配的审计框架。不同方法在决策粒度、在线搜索开销和评估协议上不一致，导致"速度更快"的结论可能被搜索开销混淆或受不严谨基线影响，需要一次严格对照的独立审计。

Method: 采用三种子、严谨匹配的审计协议，在两种模型规模与两种任务上，比较三种解码方法：置信门控早退基线（ConfLayers）、自投机解码（SWIFT）和香草自回归解码。将在线搜索开销从纯推理成本中分离以评估真实推理速度；同时对 LayerRoute 和 LayerDrop 两种训练路由方法进行补充分析，并验证其具备真实的逐输入门控、真实的完整模型基线和真实的推理期计算跳过。

Result: SWIFT 在四格中三格准确率最高，且真实推理速度在所有四格中比 ConfLayers 快 5-21%，在三个格子中逆转了朴素墙钟时间的排名；ConfLayers 在所有场景下被支配，1.5B GSM8K 上缺陷尤为明显。ConfLayers 的搜索开销小且稳定（占成本的 1-2%），SWIFT 的搜索开销更大且波动更高（最高达 28.7%）。LayerRoute 与 LayerDrop 仅带来 1.08-1.33 倍加速，准确率明显低于周期性方法，LayerRoute 在 1.5B GSM8K 上接近彻底崩坏（EM 均值 0.003）。

Conclusion: 在严格对照下，SWIFT 是周期性步进层跳转方法中更优的选择，既具备更高的准确率又拥有更快的真实推理速度，尽管其在线搜索开销更大且波动更强。层跳转方法相较传统路由方法的优势在准确率上体现明显。作者发布的审计协议为未来效率对比提供了可复用的严格模板，呼吁研究者采用"分离搜索开销、真实计算跳过、匹配基线"的评估标准。

Abstract: Layer-skipping methods for efficient LLM inference decide, at some granularity, which transformer layers to execute for a given input. We present a rigor-matched, three-seed audit of two periodic-step, search-based methods that make this decision online at inference time and re-evaluate it every few generation steps: a confidence-gated early-exit baseline (ConfLayers) and genuine self-speculative decoding (SWIFT, Xia et al. 2024), together with vanilla autoregressive decoding, across two model scales (Qwen2.5-0.5B and Qwen2.5-1.5B) and two tasks (GSM8K reasoning and CNN/DailyMail summarization). SWIFT is the strongest method on accuracy in three of four cells; ConfLayers is dominated everywhere, with particularly large deficits on GSM8K at 1.5B. Once online-search overhead is separated from pure inference cost, SWIFT's true inference speed is faster than ConfLayers's in all four cells (5-21%), reversing the naive wall-clock ranking in three of them. ConfLayers's search overhead is small and stable (1-2% of cost), while SWIFT's is larger and more variable (up to 28.7%). We additionally examine two trained-routing methods, LayerRoute (Sikdar, 2026) and LayerDrop (Fan et al. 2020), as a supplemental analysis because they operate at coarser decision granularities. Under a verified protocol with genuine per-input gating, a genuine full-model baseline, and genuine inference-time compute skipping, both show modest speedups (1.08-1.33x) but accuracy well below the periodic-step methods, including a near-total collapse for LayerRoute on GSM8K at 1.5B (0.003 mean exact-match across three seeds). We release the full audit protocol as a template for rigor-matched efficiency comparisons.

</details>


### [102] [Latent-Space Intervention for Cross-Lingual Factual Consistency: Consistency Improvements without Accuracy Drops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28860)
*Faeze Ghorbanpour,Constanza Fierro,Alexander Fraser,Anders Sogaard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究通过跨语言潜在空间干预（latent-space intervention）来减少大语言模型在不同语言间回答事实性问题的不一致性。作者训练逐层自编码器应用于平行多语言表示，并在推理时对事实性QA提示进行校正。研究发现潜在干预能改善语言间的几何对齐，并显著提升英阿、英俄等语言对的跨语言一致性（Spearman相关性提升0.16~0.20），同时不损害事实准确性。消融实验表明，AE重建带来稳定收益且无准确率代价，PCA投影贡献有限，而均值平移在开放式QA中带来更大一致性提升但以牺牲部分准确率为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）对同一个事实性问题在不同语言下常常给出不一致的回答，这种跨语言不一致性削弱了模型在多语言环境中的可靠性和事实性。本文旨在探究是否可以通过跨语言的潜在空间干预（latent-space intervention）来减少这种不一致性，从而在不牺牲事实准确性的前提下提升跨语言一致性。

Method: 作者在层级别的多语言平行表示上训练自编码器（autoencoders），然后在推理时对事实性QA提示进行干预校正。具体方法包括：(1) 在平行的多语言表示上逐层训练自编码器；(2) 在推理时对事实性QA提示应用潜在空间校正；(3) 通过消融实验比较自编码器重建、PCA投影和均值平移三种干预策略的效果。

Result: 潜在干预改善了语言间的几何对齐，并在开放式QA和多项选择QA两种格式下均带来跨语言一致性的稳定提升，且不损害事实准确性。开放式QA中，英语-阿拉伯语对的Spearman秩相关性提升0.16，英语-俄语对提升0.20。多项选择QA中，英语和非英语语言的答案一致性在KLAR和mParaRel基准上均持续提升。消融实验显示AE重建稳定有效且无准确率代价，PCA贡献微小，均值平移在开放式QA中获得更大一致性提升但牺牲部分准确率。

Conclusion: 跨语言潜在空间干预是一种有效的策略，能够在不牺牲事实准确性的前提下显著提升LLM的跨语言回答一致性。其中基于自编码器的重建方法是最佳平衡方案，而均值平移虽然能带来更大的一致性提升但需权衡准确率损失。该研究为提升多语言LLM的可信度和一致性提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often answer the same factual question differently across languages. We study whether cross-lingual latent-space intervention can reduce this inconsistency. We train layer-specific autoencoders on parallel multilingual representations and apply inference-time corrections to factual QA prompts. We find that latent intervention improves geometric alignment between languages, and that this improvement translates into consistent gains in cross-lingual consistency with English across both open-ended and multiple-choice QA formats, without degrading factual accuracy. In open-ended QA, Spearman's rank correlation between English and non-English languages improves substantially, with gains of 0.16 for English-Arabic and 0.20 for English-Russian pairs.
